### Phase 3 — The map and the candidates
- [ ] 🟥 **3.1 The gate-failure map.** A classifier in `ffl_lib.py` applying D5's tests in D5's order to every failure in Task 2.2's baseline of record (all seven sets): silent, wrong, wrongly present, the other encounter codes, and the set-level R1, R2 and R3 counts as `class_counts` returns them against rule v6's ceilings.
  - Done when: `.cursor/loops/ffl-out/map.md` holds one row per failure (set, encounter, fact or code, class) and a ranked table of classes by count; the class counts sum to the failure count; every `unknown` is listed with what the trace shows. A separate reconciliation table lists the 13 seed facts (the 12 archived silent facts and `h6-09/f1`), each with its archived state, its take-1 state and its class, or "no longer failing on take 1"; only take 1's failures count in the class sums.
- [ ] 🟥 **3.2 The candidates at audio level.** `ffl-measure.py --docs` with each candidate of Task 1.2 on, suggestions off, over take 1 of the seven sets and take 2 of the two; D8's columns; level differences within the matching noise floor marked, difference columns per take (D4).
  - Done when: `.cursor/loops/ffl-out/docs-summary.md` holds the tables; for each of the 12 archived silent facts a line says which candidates reach it at text level, on take 1 and on take 2, or that it is not failing on that take.
  - Reading note (2026-11-18, Phase 1 peer round 12 PR-LOW-601): H7's "stay-out lines newly flagged" compares lines on both sides through the alignment, so at audio a boundary drift can add a line (candidate side) or remove one (base side); the count is read against the trace's per-line ids (Task 2.3), never as an upper or lower bound.

### Phase 4 — Real speech
- [ ] 🟥 **4.1 The real-speech script, reviewed and frozen.** New `.cursor/loops/ffl-measure-kept.py` per D9, modelled on `afq-measure-kept.py` (its refusals, its numbers-only printing, its `--list`, `--enrolment` and `--config` passes, its `COMPLETE` rule). A `/review` of the script and of `ffl_lib.py`'s paths it uses; a dry run over the synthetic transcripts of Task 2.1; the refusal smoke (a non-folder, a folder with no entry, the app-running refusal as far as an agent shell can show it).
  - Done when: the dry run prints numbers only; the SHA-256 of the script and of `ffl_lib.py` are recorded here; the exact commands for the practitioner (PowerShell, one per block, both passes) are written on Task 4.2. Re-freeze rule: if Step H or any later task changes either file, the hashes are recorded again, the self-test, both premises and every table the change could touch are repeated, and the practitioner's run is repeated whenever the path it executes or a number it reports could change; otherwise neither file is edited after the hashes are recorded.
- [ ] 🟥 **4.2 The practitioner's run.** `[operator]` From a normal PowerShell with both builds closed: check the two hashes, then the two passes over the installed app's `past_sessions` (the validation cue file, then the installed app's own), each with the enrolment WAV where there is one.
  - Done when: both passes end `COMPLETE` and the practitioner has pasted the output; the executor records totals only on this task (C2): recordings measured, how many with the clinician enrolled, and the summed counts of D9. A pass that did not complete is recorded as unmeasured.

### Phase 5 — Records, hardening and the decision
- [ ] 🟥 **5.1 The records.** The document edits of D10, CRLF kept: `docs/testing/validation-harness.md` (the new section, outside the rule sections, and the one correction line in "Answer-hop simulation"), `docs/pilot/findings-register.md` (the `h6-09/f1` row; the next free F-number), `docs/testing/kept-recordings.md`, `docs/security/threat-model.md` and `docs/security/data-flow-map.md` (the one-off script named where `afq-measure-kept.py` is), and the dated reconciliation lines on `plan-answers-follow-question.md`.
  - Done when: the desktop suite is green plain (Validation / Verification); the harness section's numbers equal the scratch outputs they cite.
- [ ] 🟥 **Step H: Hardening pass** (proportional: no product code in this plan)
  - [ ] 🟥 H1: `/review-loop` over the scratch method (`ffl_lib.py`, `ffl-measure.py`, the classifier) and Task 5.1's documents, to convergence.
  - [ ] 🟥 H2: `/simplify` — recorded as not applicable unless H1 changed tracked code (none is expected).
  - [ ] 🟥 H3: `/security-review` scoped to the real-speech path (`ffl-measure-kept.py`, what it prints, what the documents say about it) and to the records being content-free.
  - [ ] 🟥 H4: a cross-family codex pass over the documents and the method as two slices, with a per-job evidence line, to confirm convergence.
- [ ] 🟥 **5.3 The exit.** `[decision]`
  - Options: (a) build a named form — the amendment adds build phases to this plan, hardened by a scoped `/review-plan`, with its thresholds fixed then and whether three enrolled kept recordings are a precondition; (b) hand the map on — no change; the next plan is plan 2 or a cause-driven plan the map points to; (c) close with no change.
  - Decide after: Tasks 3.1, 3.2 and 4.2, with the tables shown: the ranked causes, the candidates' reach and cost, the noise floor, the real-speech totals.
  - Blocks: Task 5.4; any build amendment.
- [ ] 🟥 **5.4 Close, on every exit.** The decision's dated line added to the harness section and to this plan; `/document`; on (b) or (c) the Lifecycle State and the Retained Follow-Up Items; on (a) the amendment and its scoped `/review-plan` instead of a close. The practitioner deletes `C:\ffl-sets\` (the agent shell cannot); the scratch under `.cursor/loops/` stays as this plan's method record.
  - Done when: `git status --short` shows only the files C1 allows; the commit is made on the practitioner's word; `AGENTS.md` names the outcome and the next plan.
